Title: Akty i dokumenty, otnosiashchiesia k istorii Kievskoi akademii, otdel II (1721-1795), tom I (1721-1750), chast' II: Prilozheniia, so vvedeniem i primechaniiami N. I. Petrova
Creator: The Kyiv Academy and its archbishops (the documents); N. I. Petrov (editor)
Publisher: Kyiv (no imprint line legible in the OCR)
Publication Date: before about 1918: the host record gives 1904, but the notes cite publications of 1908 and 1909 (OCR lines about 2462 and 29869), so it was printed no earlier than 1909; it is in the pre-reform orthography
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: printed between 1909 and about 1918, by its own citations and orthography; host record 1904); the archive.org record carries no rights field
Language: rus (pre-reform orthography)
Source: archive.org item akty1904_1, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (1,511,903) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: printed before 1931 on every reading, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair, garbled in places (the lists of about 1730 from about OCR line 13358). Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Documents of the Kyiv Academy, 1721-1750, with Petrov's introduction and notes: e.g. the report of Archbishop Rafail Zaborovskii of Kyiv to the Holy Synod, 30 May 1737, at about OCR line 6062.

УП. Рязанская семинария. Въ 1733 году, 40 свизибри, 
ГРцанекйй епиекоть Лаврентий Горка доносиль Со. Синоду, чтоликоды 
при Рязанекомь зраерейскомь дон учиневы въ 1794 году, но до 
1796 года учителей не бызо п обучать бызо некому, а съ феврыя 
1726 года начато было обучене священническихь и причетническихоь 
дьтей, конь по 28 сентября было 339 челибкь. Вь №797 году 
опиекопь Рязанекй Тавризь ужине выаваль къ с5% въ Ря-
